local _b = table.concat(_c)
local _k = {83, 116, 97, 110, 100, 111, 65, 110, 116, 105, 84, 97, 109, 112, 101, 114, 95, 118, 49, 49}
local _bxor = (bit32 and bit32.bxor) or (bit and bit.bxor)
assert(_bxor, "[Stando] need bit32.bxor / bit.bxor")

local function _b64(data)
    local b = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
    data = string.gsub(data, "[^" .. b .. "=]", "")
    return (string.gsub(string.gsub(data, ".", function(x)
        if x == "=" then return "" end
        local r, f = "", (string.find(b, x, 1, true) - 1)
        for i = 6, 1, -1 do
            r = r .. (f % (2 ^ i) - f % (2 ^ (i - 1)) > 0 and "1" or "0")
        end
        return r
    end), "%d%d%d?%d?%d?%d?%d?%d?", function(x)
        if #x ~= 8 then return "" end
        local c = 0
        for i = 1, 8 do
            c = c + (string.sub(x, i, i) == "1" and 2 ^ (8 - i) or 0)
        end
        return string.char(c)
    end))
end

local raw = _b64(_b)
local n = #raw
local outChars = (table.create and table.create(n)) or {}
for i = 1, n do
    local kb = _k[((i - 1) % #_k) + 1]
    outChars[i] = string.char(_bxor(string.byte(raw, i), kb))
end
local src = table.concat(outChars)

local fn, err = loadstring(src)
if not fn then
    return warn("[Stando] pack compile failed:", err)
end
local ok, err2 = pcall(fn)
if not ok then
    warn("[Stando] pack run failed:", err2)
end
